TL;DR: 本文提出Shell-LCC方法，通过建模高质量数据的流形结构作为隐式奖励信号，无需额外计算开销即可提升T2V扩散模型的视频质量，特别是在缓解低级别失真、增强高频细节方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V扩散模型依赖辅助奖励信号（如奖励模型或DPO）来对齐人类审美，但这些方法计算开销大、需要昂贵的人工标注，且对细粒度局部细节的改进有限。本文旨在寻找一种低成本、高效益的奖励信号替代方案。

Method: 基于局部坐标编码（LCC）建模高质量SFT数据的流形结构，将视频潜在表示约束在该流形上以获取密集、可微且近乎零成本的奖励信号。针对LCC存在的均值回归问题（拉向几何均值导致高频细节丢失），扩展为Shell-LCC，将流形"表面"建模为各向同性外壳，以对齐真实高密度区域。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在提升视频真实性、增强高频细节、减少过平滑伪影和缓解运动模糊方面均取得显著改进。

Conclusion: 数据流形本身可以作为一种隐式奖励模型，通过Shell-LCC建模流形表面结构能有效提升视频生成质量，为无需额外奖励信号的T2V对齐提供新思路。

Abstract: Recent text-to-video (T2V) diffusion models rely heavily on auxiliary reward signals (e.g., via reward models or DPO) to align generated content with human aesthetics and improve realism. These signals, however, incur substantial computational overhead, require costly human annotations, and often yield limited improvement in fine-grained local details. In this paper, we argue that your data manifold is secretly a reward model. By explicitly modeling the manifold structure of high-quality Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) data and encouraging video latents to lie on this manifold, we derive dense, differentiable, and nearly cost-free reward signals that significantly improve video quality, particularly in mitigating low-level distortions. Our modeling builds upon Local Coordinate Coding (LCC), which captures the `skeleton' of the manifold. However, directly applying LCC suffers from mean regression, pulling latents toward the geometric mean and losing high-frequency details. We therefore extend it to Shell Local Coordinate Coding (Shell-LCC), which models the manifold `surface' as an isotropic shell to align with the true high-density region. Experiments demonstrate that our approach improves realism, enhances high-frequency details, reduces over-smoothing artifacts, and alleviates motion blur.

</details>


### [217] [Intermediate Text Representation Guided Text-to-Image Generation for Enhancing One-and-Only Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30262)
*Soyoun Won,Aryan Yazdan Parast,Basim Azam,Jean Honorio,Naveed Akhtar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对文本到图像扩散模型中的概念关联偏差问题，提出了一种无需额外训练的中层文本表示引导方法（IR-guided diffusion），通过注入文本编码器的中间隐藏状态来恢复被抑制的概念信息，并构建了OAO-AttackBench基准来评估模型在处理反事实提示时的表现，在多个基准上实现了最高19.1个百分点的VQAScore提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型在处理“唯一对象”（如天体、地标、艺术品等具有单一规范形态的实体）时，存在强烈的概念关联偏差，导致模型倾向于生成学习到的视觉先验而忽略文本描述的显式修改，且仅靠提示工程难以克服。

Method: 通过信息论分析发现最终文本嵌入丢弃了中间层的概念级信息，导致去噪过程的互信息减少。据此提出IR-guided diffusion方法，在早期去噪步骤中将文本编码器的中间隐藏状态注入条件信号，无需额外训练、优化或外部模型即可恢复被抑制的概念。

Result: 在包括新构建的OAO-AttackBench在内的四个基准测试上，该方法在保持生成保真度和人类偏好的前提下，VQAScore最高提升了19.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 中层文本表示引导能有效缓解概念关联偏差问题，使扩散模型更好地遵循与唯一对象核心视觉身份相冲突的反事实提示，且无需额外训练成本。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models often fail to faithfully render explicit textual descriptions, instead defaulting to strongly learned visual priors due to a phenomenon referred to as concept association bias. We show that such bias is particularly strong for one-and-only (OAO) objects, entities that exist in a single canonical form, such as celestial bodies, landmarks, and artworks. The deeply ingrained visual identity for these concepts often resists modification through prompting alone. Addressing this challenge, we first identify through an information-theoretic analysis that the final text embedding discards concept-level information present in the intermediate-layer text representations, reducing the mutual information available to the subsequent denoising process. We then propose Intermediate Text Representation (IR)-guided diffusion, which injects intermediate hidden states of the text encoder into the conditioning signal during early denoising steps, recovering suppressed concepts without any additional training, optimization, or external models. To systematically evaluate the challenging task of aligning generative outputs with unusual prompts for OAO objects, we introduce OAO-AttackBench, a benchmark comprising counterfactual prompts that directly conflict with the core visual identity of OAO objects. Experiments on four benchmarks, including OAO-AttackBench, show that our method achieves up to a 19.1 percentage-point improvement in VQAScore while preserving generation fidelity and human preference. Project page: https://soyoun-won.github.io/one-and-only-ir-guidance/.

</details>


### [218] [VisReflect: Latent Visual Reflection for Fine-Grained Perception in Long Visual Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30288)
*Xiaoqian Shen,Mohamed Elhoseiny*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VisReflect框架，通过潜在空间视觉反射而非离散token解码，在单次前向传播中引导注意力聚焦于相关视觉token，有效缓解高分辨率图像和长视频中的视觉注意力下沉问题，在图像和视频基准上分别提升4.1%和1.8%，同时推理时间减少约44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLMs在处理高分辨率图像和长视频时，视觉注意力下沉现象日益严重，无关token占据了过多注意力权重；现有方法依赖边界框或时间区间预测并重新编码裁剪区域，存在离散token空间中数值定位不可靠、计算开销大的问题。

Method: 提出VisReflect框架，通过潜在视觉反射机制，生成与问题相关的连续视觉特征表示（而非离散token），在单次前向传播中引导注意力聚焦于相关视觉区域或帧。

Result: 在图像基准（BLINK、V*、HRBench-4K/8K）上提升4.1%，在视频基准（MVBench、VideoMME、MLVU）上提升1.8%；相比基于缩放的（zoom-based）方法，视频理解推理时间减少约44%，性能相当。

Conclusion: VisReflect通过潜在空间连续反射机制，有效缓解了长视觉上下文中的注意力下沉问题，在提升性能的同时显著降低计算开销，是一种简洁高效的细粒度视觉感知增强框架。

Abstract: Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable success on vision-language tasks, yet fine-grained perception over high-resolution images and long-context videos remains challenging. As the number of visual tokens increases, the visual attention sink phenomenon becomes increasingly severe, causing irrelevant tokens to absorb a disproportionate amount of attention mass. Recent approaches attempt to mitigate this issue by explicitly predicting bounding boxes or temporal spans and re-encoding the cropped visual regions. Such methods depend on unreliable numeric localization in the discrete token space and incur significant computational overhead due to additional forward passes. In this work, we propose **VisReflect**, a simple yet effective framework that improves fine-grained perception in long visual contexts through latent visual reflection. Instead of decoding intermediate predictions into discrete tokens, the model generates continuous visual reflection that represents question-relevant visual features in the latent space. These reflections selectively emphasize salient regions or frames, guiding attention towards relevant visual tokens within a single forward pass. We conduct comprehensive evaluations on challenging high-resolution image benchmarks, including BLINK, V*, and HRBench-4K/8K, as well as video understanding benchmarks such as MVBench, VideoMME, and MLVU. Our method consistently improves over strong baselines, achieving gains of 4.1% on image benchmarks and 1.8% on video benchmarks. Compared with zooming-based methods, our model achieves comparable performance while reducing inference time by roughly 44% on video understanding.

</details>


### [219] [The Surprising Effectiveness of Video Diffusion Models for Hand Motion Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30308)
*Yuxi Wang,Chengkai Jin,Yufei Liu,Wenqi Ouyang,Tianyi Wei,Zhiwei Zeng,Siyuan Huang,Zhiqi Shen,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViDiHand利用预训练视频扩散模型的表征进行4D双手姿态重建，无需检测器、填补器或测试时优化，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图像的方法在严重遮挡下失效，基于视频的方法依赖稀缺的手部姿态标注，无法充分建模运动动态、遮挡推理和手物交互。视频生成模型在互联网规模训练中隐式习得了这些能力，因此作者提出利用视频扩散模型来实现4D手部运动重建。

Method: ViDiHand采用预训练视频扩散模型，通过手部覆盖渲染目标（hand-overlay rendering objective）对其特征进行适配，使其专注于手部同时保留世界先验知识；再由解码器从适配后的特征中恢复度量尺度的姿态；整个流程直接在完整帧上运行。

Result: 在ARCTIC、HOT3D和HOI4D数据集上，ViDiHand显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 视频扩散模型可作为手部运动重建的强大新基础，并为可扩展的野外数据收集提供可行途径，助力具身AI发展。

Abstract: 4D hand motion reconstruction from egocentric video is bottlenecked by clear limitations of existing methods: image-based pipelines depend on a detector that fails under heavy occlusion, while video-based methods rely on temporal modules learned only from scarce hand-pose annotations, a narrow signal insufficient to model motion dynamics, occlusion reasoning, and hand-object interaction. These capabilities, however, are exactly what video generative models must implicitly acquire when trained to synthesize coherent video at internet scale. Motivated by this, we present ViDiHand, which leverages the representations of a pretrained video diffusion model to reconstruct 4D two-hand pose. We adapt it via a hand-overlay rendering objective that specializes its features for hands while preserving its world priors. A decoder then recovers metric-scale pose from the adapted features. The whole pipeline operates directly on full frames--no detector, no infiller, and no test-time optimization. On ARCTIC, HOT3D, and HOI4D, ViDiHand substantially outperforms prior methods, establishing video diffusion models as a powerful new foundation for hand motion reconstruction and a promising route to scalable in-the-wild data collection for embodied AI. Project page: https://vidihand.github.io.

</details>


### [220] [A Point Cloud Transformer for Remote Monitoring and Automated Assessment of Physical Rehabilitation Exercises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30309)
*Kazi Rafat,Md. Ismail Hossain,M M Lutfe Elahi,Sifat Momen,Fuad Rahman,Nabeel Mohammed,Shafin Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer的点云框架，通过分析RGBD数据中的关节点位置，自动评估康复运动质量。该框架利用曲线点云特征聚合和轴向自注意力机制，在三个基准数据集上取得优于现有方法的效果，且模型轻量、推理快、泛化能力强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有康复运动质量评估方法存在特征提取不充分、缺乏实用性、预处理成本高或忽略关键特征等问题，而传统人工监督方式成本高且难以普及。因此，需要一种低成本、可居家使用的自动评估方法。

Method: 提出基于Transformer的点云框架，使用曲线点云特征聚合技术增强点云信息，并采用轴向自注意力机制识别关键关节及其作用，从而评估康复运动质量。

Result: 在Kimore、UI-PRMD和IRDS三个基准数据集上，该框架优于现有方法，且模型体积小、推理速度快，能够在类似运动中泛化到特定关节。

Conclusion: 所提的Transformer点云评估系统兼具准确性和实用性，为康复运动的居家自动评估提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Rehabilitation exercises are essential in restoring lost physical functions of patients suffering from various diseases (e.g., Parkinson's, back pain). Carrying out these rehabilitation exercises, often prescribed by health experts, is costly, unavailable, and requires expert supervision. The availability of RGBD images and movement/position data of joints along with expert annotation of exercise data has prompted the use of automatic assessment of the quality of rehabilitation exercises, which is cost-effective and can be carried out at home. However, existing approaches do not extract relevant features, lack practical application, require expensive pre-processing, or overlook crucial features. This study proposes a transformer-based framework for point clouds to extract features and assess rehabilitation exercises by analyzing joint positions collected through RGBD data. We adapt and utilize a curve-based point-cloud feature aggregation technique to augment point-cloud information that aids model output. The transformer architecture also uses axial self-attention, recognizing important joints and their roles to assist users in performing the exercise better. The guided system outperforms existing approaches and is also practically relevant due to its small size, fast inference, and generalization on specific joints in similar exercises. We conduct our experiments on three crucial baseline datasets for rehabilitation exercises: Kimore, UI-PRMD, and IRDS.

</details>


### [221] [TRACE: A Concept Bottleneck Model for Longitudinal 3D Glioblastoma Response Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30313)
*Alia Tarek,Hamsa Saberr,Hamza Elghonemy,Youssef Afify,Tamer Basha,Omair Shahzad Bhatti,Abdulrahman M. Selim,Hasan Md Tusfiqur Alam Daniel Sonntag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACE是一个RANO 2.0对齐的概念瓶颈模型，用于可解释的纵向胶质母细胞瘤响应评估。它通过预测临床意义的肿瘤测量作为根概念，然后通过确定性规则计算下游RANO派生概念，实现了结构化的概念推理，而非直接的图像到标签预测。在LUMIERE数据集上，TRACE的四类宏F1为0.4769，二分类进展/非进展宏F1为0.7085，优于概念瓶颈基线，且与不可解释深度学习方法相当。消融实验表明专家RANO图和干预一致性训练对性能至关重要，干预实验证明修正概念可改善下游预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法直接根据影像特征预测治疗响应标签，缺乏临床可检查、可验证和可纠正的透明度。作者希望构建一个与RANO 2.0临床标准对齐的可解释模型，将响应评估建模为结构化概念推理过程，而非黑箱式的图像到标签预测。

Method: TRACE采用：1）共享3D视觉编码器处理配对的基线和随访多模态MRI；2）预测临床意义的肿瘤测量作为根概念；3）通过确定性规则计算下游RANO派生概念；4）纳入扫描间隔和新病灶信息作为直通概念。整体框架将响应评估建模为结构化概念推理。

Result: 在LUMIERE数据集上5折患者级交叉验证：四类宏F1为0.4769，二分类进展/非进展宏F1为0.7085。优于概念瓶颈基线，与不可解释深度学习方法性能范围相当。消融实验证实专家RANO图和干预一致性训练对性能重要，干预实验表明修正概念可改善预测。

Conclusion: 结构化概念瓶颈为纵向胶质母细胞瘤响应评估提供了透明且临床对齐的方向。同时指出需要更大规模的协议对齐数据集和外部验证。

Abstract: Longitudinal glioblastoma response assessment requires comparing subtle tumor changes across MRI time points using structured clinical criteria such as RANO. However, most deep learning methods predict response labels directly from imaging features, which limits clinical inspection, verification, and correction. We introduce TRACE, a RANO 2.0-aligned concept bottleneck model for interpretable 4-class glioblastoma response classification on longitudinal 3D MRI. TRACE processes paired baseline and follow-up multimodal MRI scans with a shared 3D vision encoder, predicts clinically meaningful tumor measurements as root concepts, computes downstream RANO-derived concepts through deterministic rules, and incorporates scan interval and new-lesion information as passthrough concepts. This design frames response assessment as structured concept reasoning rather than direct image-to-label prediction.
  Using 5-fold patient-wise cross-validation on the LUMIERE dataset, TRACE achieves a 4-class macro F1 of 0.4769 and a binary progression-versus-non-progression macro F1 of 0.7085. It improves over a concept bottleneck baseline and remains within the range of published non-interpretable deep learning approaches. Ablation studies show that the expert RANO graph and intervention-consistency training are important for performance, while intervention experiments demonstrate that correcting concepts can improve downstream predictions. These results suggest that structured concept bottlenecks offer a transparent and clinically aligned direction for longitudinal glioblastoma response assessment, while highlighting the need for larger protocol-aligned datasets and external validation.

</details>


### [222] [Real-Time Underwater Image Enhancement via Frequency-Guided Dual-Path Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30314)
*Leshen Zhang,Ao Li,Ce Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级水下图像增强框架FGDPA，通过多分支可重参数化卷积（MBRConv-DCT）注入固定DCT先验，并结合频域引导双路径注意力（FGDPA）融合空间与频谱特征，仅需4.23K参数即可实现600+ FPS的实时增强，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的超轻量CNN基于结构重参数化虽满足低延迟和紧凑模型需求，但仅在空间域操作，忽略了水下退化的频率敏感性，导致对频域退化处理不足。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：1）MBRConv-DCT：多分支可重参数化卷积，在训练时注入结构化的固定DCT方向频率先验，重参数化后推理零额外成本；2）FGDPA：频域引导双路径注意力模块，通过双路径设计融合空间和频谱线索实现自适应特征调制。两个组件均与结构重参数化完全兼容。

Result: 仅4.23K参数，600+ FPS推理速度，在定量指标和视觉质量上均超越参数量更大的现有方法。

Conclusion: 证明了在超轻量级网络中融合频域先验的有效性，为资源受限场景下的实时水下图像增强提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time underwater image enhancement (UIE) is crucial for mobile underwater photography and autonomous robotic systems, where practical deployment typically requires low latency and compact models under constrained computational resources. Recent ultra-lightweight CNNs based on structural re-parameterization meet these constraints but operate purely in the spatial domain, ignoring the frequency-sensitive nature of underwater degradation. To address this, we propose a lightweight UIE framework that integrates two key components: a Multi-Branch Reparameterizable Convolution with Fixed DCT Priors (MBRConv-DCT) that injects structured directional frequency priors during training, and a Frequency-Guided Dual-Path Attention (FGDPA) module that fuses spatial and spectral cues via a dual-path design for adaptive feature modulation. Both components are fully compatible with structural re-parameterization: the convolution branch introduces zero additional inference cost after re-parameterization, while the attention module incurs only a minimal computational overhead. Experiments show our model achieves state-of-the-art performance with only 4.23K parameters and 600+ FPS, outperforming much larger methods in both quantitative metrics and visual quality. Code is available at https://github.com/LethyZhang/FGDPA.

</details>


### [223] [BrainJanus: A Unified Model for Understanding and Generation across Brain, Vision, and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30319)
*Haitao Wu,Qirui Zhang,Zhouheng Yao,Shangquan Sun,Qihao Zheng,Mianxin Liu,Chi Zhang,Wanli Ouyang,Chunfeng Song,Changqing Zhang,Jiamin Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BrainJanus是首个统一大脑、视觉和语言的脑模型框架，通过统一脑分词器和全能自回归架构，实现大脑编码（图像/文本→脑）和解码（脑→图像/文本）的双向任意生成，在多项基准上取得优异性能，并具备零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑编码与解码方法被当作孤立任务处理，依赖单模态对齐和外部先验，忽视了大脑作为多模态整合系统的内在本质。

Method: 提出Unified Brain Tokenizer将连续神经活动量化为离散token，与视觉和语言表征在共享的Omni空间中对齐；采用All-in-One自回归架构，通过下一token预测实现任意模态间的生成（图像→脑、文本→脑的编码，以及脑→图像、脑→文本的解码）。

Result: 在多个基准上取得优越性能，展现出零样本泛化能力，并保留了可解释的生物拓扑结构。

Conclusion: BrainJanus作为一种通用脑建模范式，证明了将大脑视为多模态集成系统并统一建模编码与解码任务的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Modeling the bidirectional correspondence between external sensory stimuli and internal neural activity has emerged as a critical frontier in neuroscience. However, existing approaches predominantly treat brain encoding and decoding as isolated tasks, relying heavily on unimodal alignment and external priors while overlooking the brain's intrinsic nature as a multimodal integration system. To address these limitations, we propose BrainJanus, the first unified brain model that integrates brain, vision, and language within a single framework. Specifically, we introduce a Unified Brain Tokenizer to quantize continuous neural dynamics into discrete tokens aligned with visual and linguistic representations in a shared Omni space. Building on this, we utilize an All-in-One autoregressive architecture that leverages next-token prediction to enable seamless any-to-any generation, which encompasses image-to-brain and text-to-brain encoding, and brain-to-image and brain-to-text decoding. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BrainJanus achieves superior performance across diverse benchmarks. Furthermore, our framework exhibits zero-shot generalization and preserves interpretable biological topography, highlighting its potential as a general-purpose brain modeling paradigm. The code is available at \href{https://github.com/HaitaoWuTJU/BrainJanus}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [224] [Optimizing Image Preparation and Compression for Face Recognition within 1024 Bytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30321)
*Paul Andreas,Torsten Schlett,Christoph Busch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究如何在临时旅行证件中使用2D条码存储面部图像，在仅1024字节的极低存储容量下，通过优化JPEG、JPEG 2000、JPEG XL、JPEG AI、HEIF、AVIF和WebP等压缩方案，最大限度保留人脸识别性能。结果表明，JPEG AI在优化设置下人脸识别性能最佳，AVIF和WebP也表现良好，灰度预处理对ICAO合规图像比较有帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临时旅行证件缺乏机读能力，无法像普通机读旅行证件（MRTD）那样进行自动化人脸验证。2D条码是一种成本低廉的替代方案，但面临极低存储容量（1024字节）的挑战，需要探索如何在极端压缩条件下仍保持可接受的人脸识别性能。

Method: 研究多种图像压缩格式（JPEG、JPEG 2000、JPEG XL、JPEG AI、HEIF、AVIF、WebP）在目标文件大小（≤1024字节）下的表现，优化预处理步骤（灰度转换、平滑、尺寸调整）和压缩配置，分别针对ICAO合规参考样本和可能不合规的个体样本两种场景进行评估。

Result: JPEG AI在优化设置下提供了最佳人脸识别性能，尤其在高人脸图像质量的比较中表现突出；AVIF和WebP也取得良好结果；强有损压缩导致的人脸识别性能损失相对较小。灰度预处理有助于ICAO合规图像比较，而颜色保留对包含低质量样本的比较更有利；预平滑和尺寸调整也证明了其有效性。

Conclusion: 2D条码可作为临时旅行证件的可行机读方案，通过优化压缩策略（尤其采用JPEG AI）可在极低存储容量下保持较好的人脸识别性能，为临时旅行证件的自动化验证提供了有前景的低成本解决方案。

Abstract: ICAO-compliant machine readable travel documents enable automated biometric face verification. The biometric reference is stored on an RFID chip included in form of a JPEG or JPEG 2000 compressed facial image. In contrast, temporary travel documents lack of machine readability, which excludes the owner from such automated processes. This disadvantage could be solved by equipping such documents with 2D barcodes. This technology offers a resource-saving alternative to expensive RFID chips, while still offering machine readability and fast issuing processes. However, this solution introduces the challenge of storing the face images at significantly smaller storage capacities, creating the need for reducing the file size of the included facial image to a maximum of 1024 bytes. This study examines preprocessing steps and compression configurations, using JPEG, JPEG 2000, JPEG XL, JPEG AI, HEIF, AVIF, and WebP for image compression to this target size, while still preserving as much face recognition performance as possible. While the reference sample must always comply with ICAO specifications, the individual samples may or may not meet these requirements, depending on the application. This work optimizes compression steps for both of these prerequisites. It is shown that the recently standardised JPEG AI, when using optimized settings, provides the best face recognition performance, in particular when the comparison includes only images with high face image quality. AVIF and WebP also provide good results. The losses caused by the strong lossy compression are comparatively small. For the comparison of ICAO-compliant face images only, converting the images to grayscale proves to be a helpful preprocessing step, whereas for comparisons involving less suitable samples, preserving color is preferable. In addition, smoothing and resizing the images beforehand also turns out to be beneficial.

</details>


### [225] [UniGP: Taming Diffusion Transformer for Prior-Preserved Unified Generation and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30332)
*Qin Guo,Hao Luo,Dongxu Yue,Weixuan Jin,Xiao Fu,Fan Wang,Dan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniGP是一个基于MMDiT的统一框架，通过简单的联合训练将可控图像生成和密集预测任务统一起来，无需复杂的任务特定设计，在多种任务上达到或超越专门方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将扩散模型的可控生成和密集预测视为独立任务，忽略了联合建模异构分布（图像-几何对）的潜在优势，缺乏统一的框架来同时处理生成和感知任务。

Method: 基于MMDiT架构，提出DUGP（仅复制MMDiT的图像分支来建模RGB之外的密集分布）和统一数据集训练策略，通过简单联合训练学习可控生成与密集预测，无需复杂任务特定设计或损失函数。

Result: 统一模型超越先前的统一方法，性能与专门方法相当；多任务联合训练提供互补优势：生成先验丰富感知细节，感知学习改善生成的结构对齐。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模可控生成和密集预测，UniGP验证了生成与感知任务可以相互促进，为扩散模型在多任务统一框架下的应用提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion models have shown impressive performance in controllable image generation and dense prediction tasks. However, existing approaches typically treat diffusion-based controllable generation and dense prediction as separate tasks, overlooking the potential benefits of jointly modeling the heterogeneous distributions. In this work, we introduce UniGP, a framework built upon MMDiT, which unifies controllable generation and dense prediction through simple joint training, without the need for complex task-specific designs or losses, while preserving the backbone's versatile priors. By learning controllable generation and prediction under different conditions, our model effectively captures the joint distribution of image-geometry pairs. UniGP is capable of versatile controllable generation, dense prediction, and joint generation. Specifically, the proposed UniGP consists of DUGP and a unified dataset training strategy. The former, following the principle of Occam's razor, uses only a copied image branch of MMDiT to model dense distributions beyond RGB, while the latter integrates heterogeneous datasets into a unified training framework to jointly model generation and perception tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our unified model surpasses prior unified approaches and performs on par with specialized methods. Furthermore, we demonstrate that multi-task joint training provides complementary benefits: generative priors enrich perceptual details, while perceptual learning improves structural alignment in generation.

</details>


### [226] [A Classifier-Agnostic Zero-Shot Adversarial Attack Detection via CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30342)
*Hodaya Krakover,Meir Yossef Levi,Eyal Gofer,Guy Gilboa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出A⁴D，一个完全黑盒、零样本的对抗攻击检测框架，利用CLIP的提示相似度分数，在攻击和架构无关的设置下实现了SOTA检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击检测方法通常依赖攻击特定假设、需要访问对抗样本或了解底层分类器（白盒），缺乏通用且黑盒的检测方案。

Method: 利用CLIP的两个关键观察：（i）CLIP对微小不可感知的非语义扰动敏感；（ii）CLIP嵌入空间的偏移具有规律性，可作为鲁棒的攻击指标。通过基于提示的相似度分数实现零样本、黑盒检测。

Result: 在多种攻击、数据集和分类器上的实验表明，A⁴D在攻击无关和分类器无关的设置下达到了SOTA检测结果。

Conclusion: 首次将CLIP用于对抗攻击检测，验证了基于CLIP的零样本黑盒检测方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks pose a challenge to the reliability of deep learning models, motivating effective detection methods. Existing techniques often rely on attack-specific assumptions, access to adversarial samples, or knowledge of the underlying classifier (white-box). We propose \textit{$A^4D$ (\textbf{A}ttack- and \textbf{A}rchitecture-\textbf{A}gnostic \textbf{A}dversarial \textbf{D}etector)}, a completely black-box, zero-shot adversarial attack detection framework that utilizes prompt-based similarity scores derived from CLIP. To the best of our knowledge this is the first attempt to utilize CLIP for such a task. The method is based on two key observations: (i) CLIP is sensitive even to small imperceptible non-semantic perturbations; (ii) The shift in CLIP embedding space is not arbitrary and can be used as a robust attack indicator. Experiments across multiple attacks, datasets and classifiers validate that $A^4D$ achieves SOTA detection results in the attack-agnostic and classifier-agnostic setting.

</details>


### [227] [Early Cue Precision Shapes Visual Shortcut Learning in Controlled Cue-Manipulation Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30344)
*Chanho Park,Woochan Lee,Janyeong Oh,Geongho Gong,Minshu Kim,Yeachan Kwak,Seongim Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉分类器在早期学习中低层级线索的精确度如何影响其在冲突/抑制条件下的失败模式。通过合成形状-纹理任务、顺序数字训练、10类冻结表征审计和CIFAR-10自然图像纹理覆盖基准测试，发现降级但具有预测性的输入无法替代线索去相关化。在多种设置下，早期线索精确度从随机水平提升到完美水平时，冲突准确率显著下降，纹理选择率大幅上升。端到端训练显示低早期线索精确度有益于预目标冲突行为，但捷径丰富的微调会快速覆盖这一优势，因此线索去相关化需要在下游适应过程中持续维持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉分类器依赖低层级线索导致在冲突/抑制条件下失败的原因，特别是早期线索精确度（低层级线索在早期学习或下游探测拟合中预测标签的可靠性）如何塑造这一失败模式。

Method: 通过合成形状-纹理任务、顺序数字训练、10类冻结表征审计和CIFAR-10自然图像纹理覆盖基准测试等多项实验，操控物体-纹理匹配概率，评估匹配分布准确率、冲突准确率、纹理选择率和抑制行为。

Result: 降级但具有预测性的输入无法替代线索去相关化。在10类数字探测中，冲突准确率从随机线索精确度下的0.589降至目标完美纹理下的0.005；CIFAR-10冻结探测中冲突准确率从0.569降至0.114，纹理选择率从0.049升至0.855。端到端训练中低早期线索精确度改善预目标冲突行为，但捷径丰富的微调会快速覆盖这一优势。

Conclusion: 线索去相关化不能作为一次性接种处理，必须在下游适应过程中持续维持，否则捷径丰富的微调会迅速消除早期低精确度带来的冲突行为改善。

Abstract: Visual classifiers can achieve high matched-distribution accuracy while relying on low-level cues that fail under conflict or suppression. We test whether this failure is shaped by early cue precision: the reliability with which a low-level cue predicts the label during early learning or downstream probe fitting. Across synthetic shape-texture tasks, sequential digit training, a 10-class frozen-representation audit, and a CIFAR-10 natural-image-based texture-overlay benchmark, we manipulate object-texture match probability and evaluate matched-ID accuracy, conflict accuracy, texture-choice rate, and suppression behavior. Degraded-but-predictive input does not substitute for cue decorrelation. In 10-class digit probes, conflict accuracy drops from 0.589 under chance-like cue precision to 0.005 under target-perfect texture. In CIFAR-10 frozen probes, conflict accuracy drops from 0.569 to 0.114, while texture choice rises from 0.049 to 0.855; this ordering persists across texture-overlay strengths alpha in {0.15,0.25,0.35,0.50}. End-to-end CIFAR-10 training shows that low early cue precision improves pre-target conflict behavior, but shortcut-rich fine-tuning can rapidly overwrite this benefit. Cue decorrelation must therefore be maintained during downstream adaptation rather than treated as a one-time inoculation.

</details>


### [228] [FFAvatar: Feed-Forward 4D Head Avatar Reconstruction from Sparse Portrait Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30347)
*Jianjiang Yao,Ke Xian,Renxiang Dai,Robert Caiming Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FFAvatar是一个基于Transformer和3D高斯表示的前馈框架，能够从一张或多张参考肖像图像快速构建高质量、可驱动的4D头部化身，支持增量重建、交替注意力机制解耦身份与表情/视角、稀疏到稠密的学习范式，以及一个可插拔的运动细化模块用于个性化动态建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈方法需要固定数量的输入视角，缺乏灵活性，且难以在保持身份一致性的同时高效建模动态表情和视角变化。FFAvatar旨在解决这些问题，实现从任意数量参考图像快速重建高质量、可驱动的4D头部化身。

Method: 1) 基于Transformer的3D高斯框架，支持增量式重建；2) 交替注意力机制解耦身份外观与表情/视角变化；3) 稀疏到稠密的学习范式（先FLAME顶点级稀疏基元学习粗糙外观，再在UV域稠密化捕获细节）；4) 可插拔运动细化模块建模残差运动实现个性化动态。

Result: FFAvatar能够高效生成高保真、可控的4D头部化身，在灵活性、驱动效率和跨表情/视角的身份一致性渲染方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: FFAvatar通过创新的增量重建、解耦机制和稀疏到稠密策略，实现了从单张或多张参考图像快速构建高质量4D头部化身，为前馈式头部重建提供了更灵活高效的方案。

Abstract: We present FFAvatar, a Transformer-based 3D Gaussian framework for fast construction of high-quality and animatable 4D head avatars from one or more reference portrait images. Unlike existing feed-forward approaches that require a fixed number of input views, FFAvatar supports incremental reconstruction, progressively refining the avatar representation as additional reference images become available. At the core of our method is an alternating attention mechanism that disentangles identity appearance from expression and viewpoint variations, enabling the reconstruction of a canonical 3D appearance that remains consistent across poses and facial expressions. To balance visual fidelity and computational efficiency, we introduce a sparse-to-dense learning paradigm. Coarse appearance features are first learned using sparse primitives anchored to the FLAME vertex level and are subsequently densified in the UV domain to capture fine-grained geometric and texture details. We further propose a plug-and-play motion refinement module that enables subject-specific dynamic personalization by modeling residual motion beyond parametric deformation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FFAvatar efficiently produces high-fidelity and controllable 4D head avatars, achieving superior flexibility, driving efficiency, and identity-consistent rendering across diverse expressions and viewpoints.

</details>


### [229] [FastPano3D: Feed-Forward Indoor Panoramic 3D Reconstruction from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30352)
*Jianqiang Li,Liumei Zhang,Wenjia Guo,Tianlong Feng,Yongzhi Liao,Di Lu,Hanchi Ren,Jingjing Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FastPano3D是一个端到端框架，能从单张全景图直接生成可渲染的3D高斯表示，无需测试时优化，在秒级内重建高保真3D室内场景，推理速度比此前最优方法快156倍，参数量仅为其一半，渲染质量与NeRF和3DGS方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景重建在渲染质量、推理效率和数据依赖性之间存在复杂权衡，尤其是从少量输入（如单张全景图）快速重建详细3D室内场景面临挑战。全景图本身存在等距柱状投影畸变和特征分布不均匀的问题，而现有3DGS方法依赖多视图监督或每场景优化，效率低下。

Method: FastPano3D包含三个核心组件：轻量级特征编码器、自适应高斯采样策略和点云引导的精炼策略。该框架以端到端方式从单张全景图直接前馈生成3D高斯表示，完全无需测试时优化。

Result: 在数秒内完成高保真3D场景重建，推理速度比Pano2Room等最先进方法快156倍，参数量仅为其一半，渲染质量与基于NeRF和3DGS的重建方法相当。

Conclusion: FastPano3D为单视图快速3D场景推理建立了新的基准，有效解决了全景图直接生成3D高斯表示的畸变和特征非均匀分布问题，在效率与质量之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D scene reconstruction have highlighted the intricate trade-offs among rendering quality, inference efficiency, and data dependency. To address the challenge of rapidly reconstructing detailed 3D indoor scenes from minimal input, we introduce FastPano3D, an end-to-end framework that directly generates renderable 3D Gaussian representations from a single panoramic image. Unlike perspective-based methods, panoramic images inherently suffer from equirectangular projection distortions and spatially non-uniform feature distributions, making direct feed-forward Gaussian generation particularly challenging. In contrast to existing Gaussian Splatting based methods that rely on multi-view supervision or per-scene optimization, FastPano3D employs a lightweight feature encoder, adaptive Gaussian sampling, and a point-cloud-guided refinement strategy to achieve efficient and accurate scene generation without any test-time optimization. Our approach reconstructs high-fidelity 3D scenes within seconds, achieving up to 156 times faster inference than prior state-of-the-art methods such as Pano2Room, while using only half the parameters. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FastPano3D delivers rendering quality comparable to NeRF- and 3DGS-based reconstructions, establishing a new benchmark for rapid, single-view 3D scene inference.

</details>


### [230] [Residual-Guided Expert Specialization for Incomplete Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30355)
*Seunghun Baek,Jihwan Park,Jaeyoon Sim,Minjae Jeong,Hoseok Lee,Won Hwa Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MARS是一个基于混合专家模型的框架，通过缺失感知的残差引导专家 specialization，解决不完整多模态学习问题，在分类和分割任务上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实预测系统常面临推理时模态缺失问题，现有方法虽致力于学习对缺失输入鲁棒的表示，但不完整模态的表示与完整模态表示之间存在偏差，现有方法未能显式利用这种偏差。

Method: 提出MARS框架：1）通过对比不完整输入与完整输入的任务表示，推导出特权残差信号捕获表示间隙；2）残差路由器根据该信号将样本分配给对应偏差模式的专家；3）特征路由器仅用不完整输入模仿残差路由器的行为；4）开发差异感知噪声正则化，在特征路由器偏离时自适应扰动残差路由器决策以增强专家鲁棒性。

Result: 在CASIA-SURF、CREMA-D、UPMC Food-101（分类）和MCubeS（分割）等多模态数据集上，MARS在模态缺失场景下一致超越基线，同时保持高效性并兼容多种骨干网络和任务。

Conclusion: MARS通过显式利用缺失导致的表示偏差，引导专家专业化，有效解决了不完整多模态学习问题，且具有良好的效率和可扩展性。

Abstract: As real-world prediction systems often face missing modalities at inference, incomplete multimodal learning (IML) remains a practical challenge. While prior methods aim to learn representations robust to missing inputs, representations from incomplete modalities inevitably deviate from their full-modality counterparts due to missing evidence. To explicitly leverage these deviations, we propose MARS (Missingness-Aware Residual-guided Specialization), a mixture-of-experts framework that guides expert specialization based on how representations are reshaped by missingness. By contrasting task representations derived from incomplete inputs with their complete counterparts during training, we derive a privileged residual signal that captures this representational gap. The residual signal guides a residual router to assign samples to experts specialized for the corresponding deviation patterns. In parallel, a feature router learns to imitate this routing behavior using only incomplete inputs, enabling deployment without access to full modalities. To mitigate this train-test router gap, we develop a discrepancy-aware noise regularization that adaptively perturbs the residual router's decisions when the feature router deviates, enhancing expert robustness under imperfect imitation. Experiments on multimodal classification (CASIA-SURF, CREMA-D, UPMC Food-101) and segmentation (MCubeS) under missing scenarios show that MARS consistently surpasses baselines while remaining efficient and extensible to diverse backbones and tasks.

</details>


### [231] [CouCE: A Unified Causal Framework for Debiased Deep Metric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30365)
*Xin Yuan,Zhenyang Niu,Meiqi Wan,Huilin Zhu,Xin Xu,Kui Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Counterfactual Causal Embedding (CouCE)框架，通过正交字典后门调整(ODBA)和多尺度随机因果干预(MSRCI)分别消除背景虚假相关和前景扰动两种混杂因素，在不修改推理架构的情况下实现深度度量学习的去偏，在多个数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度度量学习(DML)方法受限于两种结构上不同的混杂因素——背景虚假相关（通过场景上下文产生后门路径）和前景扰动（姿态、光照等非语义变化），而现有方法只能针对单一路径进行优化，无法同时解决两者。

Method: 提出CouCE统一因果框架：1) ODBA利用方差门控字典分离背景模式，通过软正交正则化解耦嵌入；2) MSRCI通过多尺度傅里叶振幅随机化和对称KL不变性约束，强制模型对前景扰动保持因果不变性。该方法可无缝集成任何基于代理的损失函数。

Result: 在CUB-200-2011、Cars-196和Stanford Online Products数据集上，CouCE一致达到最先进性能，且仅增加少量训练开销，推理时不需修改架构。

Conclusion: CouCE为去偏深度度量学习提供了一个原则性且鲁棒的解决方案，首次在统一框架中同时处理背景和前景两类混杂因素。

Abstract: Deep Metric Learning (DML) often struggles with zero-shot generalization because standard objectives inherently capture what co-occurs rather than what causes similarity. Consequently, DML models are vulnerable to shortcut learning driven by two structurally distinct confounders: background spurious correlations (which create backdoor paths via scene context) and foreground nuisance perturbations (which inject non-semantic variations like pose or illumination). Although existing methods have proposed targeted solutions for each pathway individually, none can simultaneously address both due to their fundamentally distinct causal roles. To bridge this gap, we propose the Counterfactual Causal Embedding (CouCE), a unified causal framework that explicitly models and neutralizes both confounders. Specifically, we introduce Orthogonal Dictionary-Based Backdoor Adjustment (ODBA), which isolates spurious background patterns into a variance-gated dictionary and stably disentangles them from the learned embeddings via soft orthogonal regularization. Simultaneously, we propose Multi-Scale Randomized Causal Intervention (MSRCI) to enforce causal invariance against foreground nuisances through multi-scale Fourier amplitude randomization and a symmetric KL invariance constraint. Notably, CouCE seamlessly integrates with any proxy-based loss, incurring modest training overhead without requiring architectural modifications during inference. Extensive experiments on CUB-200-2011, Cars-196, and Stanford Online Products demonstrate that CouCE consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance, providing a principled and robust solution for debiased DML.

</details>


### [232] [MUSE: Unlocking Timestep as Native Task Steering for One-Step Dense Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30370)
*Shuo Zhou,Zhaoxin Li,Xiujuan Chai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现单步扩散模型中固定的正弦时间步嵌入可被重新用作内生任务引导信号，据此提出MUSE方法，无需额外参数即可在单模型中同时完成深度估计与法线估计等多任务密集预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的多任务学习方法通常引入大量额外参数（如适配器、专家模块或可学习任务标记），导致计算冗余，亟需一种更高效的参数无关的多任务统一框架。

Method: 揭示预训练单步扩散模型中固定的正弦时间步嵌入可内生地作为任务引导信号；提出MUSE方法，利用不同时间步值将生成过程引导至潜在空间中解耦的特定任务流形，无需引入额外参数。

Result: 在10个数据集上的实验表明，MUSE在单目深度估计和法线估计任务上均取得极具竞争力的性能，且其有效性可泛化至U-Net和DiT两种架构。

Conclusion: 本工作通过解锁现有生成基础设施的潜在能力，为构建通用视觉模型提供了一条简洁且高效的路径。

Abstract: Monocular dense prediction has recently seen remarkable success by repurposing pre-trained diffusion models. This opens a promising yet challenging avenue for more efficient multi-task learning paradigm. However, existing multi-task diffusion methods often introduce parameter-heavy adapters, experts, or learnable task tokens, leading to computational redundancy. In this paper, we reveal an inherent mechanism within one-step diffusion models: the native, fixed sinusoidal timestep embedding can be repurposed as an endogenous task steering signal. Based on this discovery, we propose Multi-task Unified eStimation via timestep Embedding (MUSE), a parameter-free, single-model multi-tasking approach for dense prediction. We interpret this mechanism via Manifold Decoupling, where discrete, fixed timestep values deterministically steer the generation process towards decoupled, task-specific manifolds in the latent space. Extensive experiments across 10 datasets demonstrate that MUSE achieves highly competitive performance on both monocular depth and normal estimation, and its efficacy generalizes across U-Net and DiT architectures. Our work offers a concise and efficient path toward generalist vision models by simply unlocking the latent potential of existing generation infrastructure.

</details>


### [233] [Set-Inclusive Uncertainty Modeling for Robust Brain Tumor Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30374)
*Seunghun Baek,Jihwan Park,Jaeyoon Sim,Hoseok Lee,Seungjoo Lee,Won Hwa Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多模态MRI缺失场景，提出概率表征框架，将特征建模为高斯分布，均值捕获任务信息，方差衡量缺失证据的不确定性，并通过集合包含策略维护模态子集间一致的不确定性关系，在BraTS数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态MRI对脑肿瘤分割至关重要，但推理时部分模态常缺失，导致信息损失带来的固有不确定性。现有方法将不完整证据编码为确定性表征，虽看似合理但缺乏可靠性。

Method: 1) 将特征建模为高斯分布，均值捕获任务信息，方差衡量缺失证据的不确定性；2) 正则化各部分配置的均值向全模态均值靠拢，并用对齐均值的差异缩放方差；3) 引入集合包含策略，利用模态子集的层级结构施加排序约束，维护一致的不确定性关系。

Result: 在BraTS 2018和2020数据集上，该方法在多种缺失模态场景下均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提概率表征框架能有效建模缺失模态下的不确定性，提升分割可靠性与性能。

Abstract: Multimodal MRI is essential for accurate brain tumor segmentation. However, acquiring all modalities at inference is often challenging in practice, which causes intrinsic uncertainty due to unavoidable information loss. Without modeling this uncertainty, existing methods encode incomplete evidence into deterministic representations that appear plausible but lack reliability. In this regime, we propose a probabilistic representation framework that models representations as Gaussian distributions, where their mean captures task information and their variance measures uncertainty from missing evidence. To make variance reflect information deficiency, we regularize the mean from each partial configuration toward its full-modality counterpart, while scaling the variance with the discrepancy between their aligned means. We further introduce a set-inclusive strategy that exploits the hierarchical structure of modality subsets and enforces an ordering constraint to maintain their consistent uncertainty relationships. Extensive experiments on BraTS 2018 and 2020 demonstrate that our approach offers superior performance over baselines across diverse missing-modality scenarios. Code and model checkpoint are available at https://github.com/atlas-sky/SIUM.

</details>


### [234] [OmniCoT: A Benchmark for Global and Multi-Step Panoramic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30378)
*Haocong He,Chenfei Liao,Zichen Wen,Zihao Dongfang,Xu Zheng,Bin Ren,Chang Su,Zixin Zhang,Harold Haodong Chen,Hongfei Zhang,Weijia Li,Kailun Yang,Conghui He,Xuming Hu,Nicu Sebe,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniCoT全景空间推理套件，包含评估基准(OmniCoT-B/Real)和训练数据(OmniCoT-T)，通过结构化思维链标注和两阶段训练策略(SFT+GRPO)，增强多模态大模型在全景图像中的全局多步空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全景基准测试多依赖局部线索或单/少步推理，未能充分利用全景图360°×180°视场角所支持的复杂全局多步推理这一根本优势，亟需一个能对齐全景图像固有能力的空间推理评估与训练方案。

Method: 构建OmniCoT套件：(1) OmniCoT-B (6.7K)评估数据，衡量答案准确率和推理质量；(2) OmniCoT-Real (1K)人工标注真实子集量化Sim-to-Real差距；(3) OmniCoT-T (14.3K)训练数据，含结构化逐步思维链标注，显式链接中间推理步骤与全景证据。基于OmniCoT-T提出OmniCoT-R1，采用两阶段训练策略：SFT将推理锚定到全景证据(如方位、临近性)，GRPO惩罚几何不连贯路径以巩固全局360°空间一致性。

Result: OmniCoT提供了评估数据集和训练数据集，并通过两阶段训练策略提升MLLM在全景空间推理任务上的表现，同时量化了仿真到真实环境的性能差距。

Conclusion: OmniCoT重新校准了全景空间推理的难度标准，使其更好地对齐全景图像的内在能力，推动该研究领域取得有意义的进展。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated promising spatial reasoning capabilities, while these abilities remain underexplored in the emerging visual modality of panoramic imagery. The full 360°$\times$180° field of view of panoramas essentially supports complex global multi-step reasoning, which is also the fundamental advantage of panoramas in applications such as embodied intelligence. However, existing panoramic benchmarks largely focus on simplistic queries that rely on local cues or single-/few-step reasoning, thereby ignoring the fundamental advantage of panoramas and failing to fully exploit their potential. To address this gap, we introduce OmniCoT, a panoramic spatial reasoning suite designed to enable MLLMs to use global evidence and perform multi-step inference across viewpoints. It includes OmniCoT-B (6.7K data) for evaluation, which measures both answer accuracy and reasoning quality, OmniCoT-Real (1K data) as a manually annotated real-world subset to quantify the Sim-to-Real gap. For training, OmniCoT-T (14.3K data) is purpose-built with structured stepwise Chain-of-Thought annotations that explicitly link intermediate reasoning steps to panoramic evidence. Based on OmniCoT-T, we introduce OmniCoT-R1 and adopt a two-stage training strategy tailored to the geometrically complex panoramic space, where Supervised Fine-tuning (SFT) anchors reasoning to panoramic evidence (e.g., bearings, proximity) and GRPO penalizes geometrically incoherent paths to consolidate global 360° spatial consistency. Through OmniCoT, we aim to recalibrate the difficulty of panoramic spatial reasoning to better align with the intrinsic capabilities of panoramic imagery, thereby fostering meaningful progress in this research area.

</details>


### [235] [SADL: What to Ignore? A Benchmark for Subject-Aware Distractor Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30393)
*Cao-Tri Nguyen,Nguyen-Khoa Luong,Vinh-Tiep Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SADL，首个面向主体感知的干扰物定位基准数据集，包含1,800个主体感知用例和14,617个带标注的候选对象，用于系统评估视觉语言模型在干扰物分类和排除过滤方面的能力。实验发现VLM在识别干扰物方面表现出色，但过度应用排除规则，系统性地抑制了真正的干扰物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有照片编辑工具虽能移除对象，但识别哪些对象需要移除仍需人工操作；现有显著性模型和开放词汇检测器缺乏主体感知能力，无法适应不同用户意图；且脱离上下文的移除会破坏场景语义连贯性（如移除椅子导致坐椅子上的人悬空）。

Method: 构建了SADL基准数据集（1,000张照片、1,800个主体感知用例、14,617个标注候选对象，含1,938个硬负例），设计了包含五种包含因素和三种上下文排除规则的顺序流水线（干扰物分类→排除过滤），评估了7个VLM模型。

Result: VLM在识别干扰物方面能力强，但在排除阶段过度应用规则，系统性地抑制了真正干扰物，暴露了主体条件推理的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: SADL作为基础诊断工具，能够推进多模态系统中的主体条件推理能力，为未来研究提供系统评估框架。

Abstract: Photographs frequently contain \emph{visual distractors} besides foregrounds and backgrounds of the intended subject, competing for attention and weakening composition. While modern editing tools streamline object removal, identifying which objects to remove remains a mostly manual process. Existing saliency models and open-vocabulary detectors operate without subject awareness, failing to adapt to shifting user intent. Furthermore, context-agnostic removal may disrupt the scene's semantic coherence (e.g., keep the person but remove the chair they are sitting on). To address these limitations, we formalize the task of subject-aware distractor localization, which identifies distractors while retaining compositionally essential objects. This paper introduces \textsc{SADL}, the first real-world benchmark for this task, comprising 1,800 subject-aware cases across 1,000 photographs to enable systematic evaluation and facilitate future research. In total, there are 14,617 annotated candidates, including a robust set of 1,938 hard negatives to stress-test exclusion calibration. We evaluate seven proprietary and open-weight Vision-Language Models (VLMs) on a sequential pipeline of distractor classification followed by exclusion filtering, structured around five inclusion factors and three contextual exclusion rules. Our analysis reveals that VLMs are highly capable of identifying distractors, but then over-apply exclusion, which systematically suppresses true distractors at scale. By exposing this critical bottleneck, \textsc{SADL} provides a foundational diagnostic tool to advance subject-conditioned reasoning in multimodal systems.

</details>


### [236] [SA-Homo: Scale Adaptive Homography Estimation for Scale Variation Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30408)
*Shangxuan Xie,Haifeng Wu,Yuhang Wang,Huarong Jia,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SA-Homo，一种尺度自适应单应性估计框架，通过分层尺度对齐策略（全局粗对齐+局部细调）解决大尺度差异下的鲁棒对齐问题，在8倍尺度差异下仍保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习单应性估计方法在尺度变化场景下性能显著下降，因为它们依赖于有限位移假设和局部特征一致性，这些假设在大尺度差距下不成立。

Method: 采用分层尺度对齐策略：(1) 全局阶段：SDBM模块通过MLAC捕获长程依赖、CSMB进行尺度鲁棒的关联表示；(2) 局部阶段：轻量级IHERM利用局部相关性逐步细化结果。同时贡献了高分辨率多模态卫星数据集HMSA。

Result: 在8倍尺度差异下仍保持高精度，在常规尺度相似场景和挑战性尺度变化场景中均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SA-Homo通过粗到精的分层尺度对齐策略有效解决了单应性估计中的尺度变化挑战，在大范围尺度差异下实现鲁棒对齐。

Abstract: Homography estimation, as one of the fundamental problems in computer vision, remains challenged by scale variation scenarios where image pairs potentially exhibit significant scale discrepancies. Existing deep learning frameworks frequently suffer from a significant performance degradation in such cases, as they rely on limited displacement assumptions and local feature consistency that might not hold under large scale gaps. In this paper, we propose SA-Homo, a novel scale-adaptive homography estimation framework designed to achieve robust alignment across a wide range of scale discrepancy ratios. We adopt a hierarchical scale alignment strategy that transitions from the global perspective with a heavy module to a local perspective with a light module. Specifically, we introduce the Scale-aware Discrepancy Bridging Module (SDBM) for initial alignment, which utilizes a Multi-scale Linear Attention Cascade (MLAC) to capture long-range dependencies and mitigate feature inconsistencies, along with a global Cross-scale Similarity Matrix Block (CSMB) for scale robust correlation representation. Once the initial scale gap is bridged, a lightweight Iterative Homography Estimation Refinement Module (IHERM) progressively polishes the result using local correlations. To facilitate this research, we contribute the HMSA dataset, a high-resolution, multi-modal satellite benchmark specifically tailored for scale-variant challenges. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SA-Homo maintains high precision even under 8$\times$ scale discrepancies, outperforming state-of-the-art methods in both conventional scale-similar scenarios and challenging scale variation scenarios. Code and collected datasets are available at https://github.com/shangxuanx330/SA_Homo

</details>


### [237] [Beyond Point Estimates for Glaucoma Visual Field Forecasting with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30417)
*Marta Colmenar Herrera,Pablo Márquez Neila,Şerife Seda Kucur Ergünay,Martin S. Zinkernagel,Raphael Sznitman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出使用条件去噪扩散模型进行视野概率预测，生成未来视野的分布以表征不确定性。在两个独立数据集上，该方法产生了良好校准的分布，在点估计任务上达到最先进精度，推动了从点估计预测向不确定性感知的临床风险评估的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青光眼视野预测对个性化监测和治疗规划至关重要，但由于疾病进展异质性和测量变异性，预测存在固有不确定性。现有方法大多产生单一确定性预测，无法表征这种不确定性。

Method: 将视野预测表述为概率预测问题，使用条件去噪扩散模型从不规则随访间隔的纵向观测中生成未来视野的概率分布。

Result: 在两个独立视野数据集上，扩散模型预测产生良好校准的分布；在简化为标准点估计时，与临床基线和先前学习方法相比达到最先进精度。

Conclusion: 分布建模在视野预测中具有优势，应推动从点估计预测向不确定性感知、临床可解释的风险评估转变。

Abstract: Forecasting visual fields (VFs) is critical for personalized monitoring and treatment planning in glaucoma. This is inherently uncertain due to heterogeneous disease progression and measurement variability, yet most existing methods produce single deterministic predictions that fail to represent this uncertainty. We formulate VF forecasting as a probabilistic prediction problem and the use of conditioned denoising diffusion models to generate distributions of plausible future VFs from longitudinal observations with irregular follow-up intervals. Experiments on two independent VF cohorts show that diffusion-based predictions produce well-calibrated distributions for clinically relevant VF measures. When reduced to a standard point-estimate, the proposed approach achieves state-of-the-art accuracy compared to clinical baselines and prior learning-based methods. Our results highlight the advantages of distributional modeling for VF forecasting and support a shift from point-estimate prediction toward uncertainty-aware, clinically interpretable risk assessment in glaucoma.

</details>


### [238] [OWMDrive: Causality-Aware End-to-End Autonomous Driving via 4D Occupancy World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30421)
*Junjie Cheng,Ruiqi Song,Ye Wu,Nanxing Zeng,Ximiao Li,Yunfeng Ai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OWMDrive是一种基于占据世界模型的多步3D占据预测生成式端到端自动驾驶框架，通过显式建模场景时空因果演化来引导扩散规划器生成更稳健、有前瞻性的轨迹，在复杂和部分可观测场景中显著提升了规划可靠性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶方法仍依赖当前场景的静态表征进行决策，缺乏对未来演变的显式建模和对交通交互中时序因果动态的建模，导致在遮挡、突发事件等高不确定性条件下出现不稳定或过于保守的规划。

Method: 基于占据世界模型进行多步3D占据预测，将预测的未来状态作为条件先验，结合当前观测输入扩散规划器，通过迭代精炼轨迹候选生成增强的驾驶轨迹。

Result: OWMDrive在规划可靠性和安全性方面显著提升，尤其是在具有挑战性和部分可观测的驾驶场景中表现优异。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模未来时间范围内的场景演化，捕获关键的时空因果依赖关系，能够生成更具前瞻性和鲁棒性的轨迹。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems are steadily moving toward end-to-end paradigms to mitigate the limited adaptability of rule-based pipelines in complex traffic environments. However, most existing learning-based methods still make decisions from static representations of the current scene, without explicit future rollouts or modeling of the temporal causal dynamics in traffic interactions. This limitation often results in unstable or overly conservative planning under high-uncertainty conditions, such as occlusions and unexpected events. To overcome these challenges, we introduce OWMDrive, a generative end-to-end driving framework built upon an Occupancy World Model for multi-step 3D occupancy forecasting, which serves as a conditional prior to guide diffusion-based planning. Conditioned on both current observations and predicted future states, the planner iteratively refines trajectory candidates to generate a reinforced driving trajectory. By explicitly modeling scene evolution over future horizons, OWMDrive captures key spatiotemporal causal dependencies, which leads to more foresighted and robust trajectory generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OWMDrive significantly improves planning reliability and safety, especially in challenging and partially observable driving scenarios.

</details>


### [239] [Robust and Efficient Monocular 3D Gaussian SLAM for Kilometer-Scale Outdoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30436)
*Sicheng Yu,Dongxu Shen,Beizhen Zhao,Guanzhi Ding,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KiloGS-SLAM提出了一种面向千米级室外环境的单目3DGS-SLAM系统，通过运动自适应混合跟踪模块（条件触发的三级求解管线，应对几何退化与轨迹漂移）和生命周期管理的高斯映射策略（概率初始化+基于块的稠密化与剪枝），在单GPU上实现超万帧序列的鲁棒跟踪与高质量渲染，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将单目3DGS-SLAM扩展到千米级室外环境面临两个紧密耦合的挑战：长期位姿跟踪的脆弱性，以及大规模建图过程中的内存开销过大。

Method: 1) 运动自适应混合跟踪模块：条件触发的三级求解管线，在本质矩阵与PnP模型间动态切换以处理几何退化，并可激活按需基础模型挽救轨迹漂移；2) 生命周期管理的高斯映射策略：概率初始化结合基于块的多视图稠密化与剪枝，实现全管线优化，减少图元冗余同时保留高频细节。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的室外数据集上，该方法在跟踪精度和渲染质量上达到SOTA，成功在单GPU上扩展到超过10,000帧的序列。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的跟踪保证了精确建图的几何基础，而内存高效的生命周期管理建图使大规模操作成为可能，二者协同解决了室外大规模3DGS-SLAM的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Scaling monocular 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) SLAM to kilometer-level outdoor environments poses two tightly coupled challenges: fragile long-term pose tracking and excessive memory overhead during large-scale mapping. In this paper, we propose KiloGS-SLAM, a highly efficient and robust monocular 3DGS-SLAM system that jointly addresses both bottlenecks. Since high-fidelity scene reconstruction fundamentally relies on drift-free camera poses, we first introduce a motion-adaptive hybrid tracking module. This module features a condition-triggered three-tier solving pipeline. It dynamically switches between Essential matrix and PnP models to handle geometric degeneracies. An on-demand foundation model can also be activated to rescue the trajectory from catastrophic drift. To ensure the system can sustain these long trajectories without memory exhaustion, we subsequently design a lifecycle-managed Gaussian mapping strategy. By integrating probabilistic initialization with chunk-based multi-view densification and pruning, this full-pipeline optimization effectively reduces primitive redundancy while preserving high-frequency details. Together, the robust tracking guarantees the geometric foundation required for accurate mapping, while the memory-efficient lifecycle-managed mapping enables large-scale operation. Extensive experiments across three challenging outdoor datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art tracking accuracy and rendering quality, successfully scaling to sequences of over 10,000 frames on a single GPU.

</details>


### [240] [Cross-Resolution Semantic Transfer for Robust Text-to-Image Retrieval in Low-Resolution Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30458)
*Wenjie Qian,Bin Yang,Xiao Wang,Wenke Huang,Ling Mei,Xin Xu,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出跨分辨率语义迁移（CRST）框架，通过分辨率条件推理、文本引导细化与CR-RDA模块，解决文本到图像行人重识别中因分辨率变化导致的证据可靠性崩溃与排序分布漂移问题，在三个基准数据集上显著提升低分辨率检索性能且不损失高分辨率精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像行人重识别方法忽略了真实监控场景中的分辨率差异，导致低分辨率下视觉特征退化、文本对齐失效，以及混合分辨率图库中排序不稳定。

Method: 提出CRST框架，包含三个模块：1）分辨率条件推理——估计token可靠性以抑制退化证据；2）文本引导细化——注入语义先验恢复判别线索；3）CR-RDA——迁移高分辨率邻域几何结构以稳定低分辨率排序。

Result: 在CUHK-PEDES、ICFG-PEDES和RSTPReid数据集上，超低分辨率下Rank-1和mAP平均提升5.7%和5.3%，混合分辨率检索稳定且不牺牲高分辨率精度。

Conclusion: CRST有效解决了跨分辨率场景下TIPR的两个关键失败模式（ERC和RDD），为实际监控场景中的跨分辨率行人检索提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Text-to-image person re-identification (TIPR) retrieves target persons using natural language descriptions. However, existing methods largely overlook resolution variance in real-world surveillance. They characterize cross-resolution TIPR through two coupled failure modes: Evidence Reliability Collapse (ERC), where degraded visual tokens become unreliable for grounding fine-grained text, and Ranking Distribution Drift (RDD), where mixed-resolution galleries distort similarity neighborhoods and destabilize retrieval rankings. To address this challenge, we propose Cross-Resolution Semantic Transfer (CRST), a CLIP-style framework with three modules: resolution-conditioned reasoning, text-guided refinement and CR-RDA. Resolution-conditioned reasoning estimates token reliability to suppress corrupted evidence. Text-guided refinement injects semantic priors to recover discriminative cues. CR-RDA transfers HR neighborhood geometry to stabilize LR ranking under mixed resolutions. Experiments on CUHK-PEDES, ICFG-PEDES, and RSTPReid show that CRST improves ultra-low-resolution Rank-1 and mAP on average by 5.7% and 5.3%, while stabilizing mixed-resolution retrieval without sacrificing high-resolution accuracy.The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [241] [FR-DETR: Frequency and Recurrent Feature Refinement for Robust Object Detection under Adverse Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30471)
*Tuan-Duc Nguyen,Duc-Trong Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FR-DETR提出了一种以检测器为中心的框架，通过频率精化模块和循环焦点精化模块在特征层面进行增强，而非在图像层面级联增强器，从而在恶劣天气下实现高效且准确的目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于增强器的方法通过级联增强器和检测器来应对恶劣天气下的目标检测，但这种方法引入了冗余的特征提取，计算成本高，且与SOTA检测器配合时精度提升有限。

Method: 提出FR-DETR框架，包含两个核心模块：(I) 频率精化模块(Frequency Refinement Module)，动态分离并重新加权低高频分量以增强前景-背景区分度；(II) 循环焦点精化模块(RFRM)，利用粗预测结果迭代地精化特征。

Result: FR-DETR在恶劣天气条件下实现了优越的检测精度，同时计算效率显著高于基于增强器的方法。

Conclusion: 以检测器为中心、在特征层面而非图像层面进行增强的方法，能够在保持高效计算的同时有效提升恶劣天气下的目标检测性能。

Abstract: Object detection under adverse weather remains challenging due to severe visual degradations and domain shifts. Existing enhancer-based approaches attempt to improve detection by cascading an enhancer with a detector, but they introduce redundant feature extraction and incur high computational cost with limited accuracy gains when paired with SOTA detectors. We propose FR-DETR, a detector-centric framework that refines features rather than images, focusing enhancement on regions of interest and leveraging frequency-domain cues. Specifically, we design (I) a Frequency Refinement Module that dynamically separates and reweights low- and high-frequency components to improve foreground-background discrimination, and (II) a Recurrent Focus Refinement Module (RFRM) that iteratively refines features using coarse predictions as guidance. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FR-DETR achieves superior detection accuracy under adverse weather while being significantly more computationally efficient than enhancer-based methods. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/ducnt1210/FR-DETR.

</details>


### [242] [PS-MOT: Cultivating Instance Awareness from Point Seeds for Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30476)
*Kai Luo,Fei Teng,Mengfei Duan,Wanjun Jia,Xu Wang,Hao Shi,Kunyu Peng,Zhiyong Li,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PS-Track，一种从点标注到实例跟踪的分层框架，通过数据层的时序反馈提示、模型层的点激发小波注意力和损失层的不确定性引导高斯学习，有效解决了点监督多目标跟踪中的空间模糊和身份漂移问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多目标跟踪依赖边界框标注成本高昂，点监督作为一种低成本替代方案，但面临空间模糊性和身份漂移等挑战，原因是缺乏显式几何结构和尺度约束。

Method: PS-Track采用分层流水线：(1) 数据层：TFP利用负空间线索和运动先验将点演化为时序一致的伪标签；(2) 模型层：PEWA模块利用语义相关性激活高频分量，"幻觉"出物体边界；(3) 损失层：UGL将伪标签建模为概率分布，动态校准监督强度。

Result: 在DanceTrack、EmboTrack、SportsMOT和JRDB数据集上，PS-Track在点监督跟踪任务中达到新SOTA，证明了其在多样化跟踪场景中的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: PS-Track提供了一种可行的点监督替代方案，将关注点从空间拟合转向拓扑中心驱动表示，为低成本多目标跟踪开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We introduce Point-supervised Multi-Object Tracking (PS-MOT) as a cost-effective alternative to traditional bounding box supervision, shifting the focus from spatial fitting to topological center-driven representation. However, PS-MOT faces challenges, e.g., spatial ambiguity and identity drift due to the lack of explicit geometric structure and scale constraints. To address these, we propose PS-Track, a hierarchical pipeline transitioning from points to instances across data, model, and loss levels. At the data level, we introduce Temporal-Feedback Prompting (TFP) to evolve points into temporally consistent pseudo-labels using negative spatial cues and motion priors. At the model level, we design the Point-Excited Wavelet Attention (PEWA) module, which leverages semantic correlations to activate high-frequency components, ``hallucinating'' object boundaries. At the loss level, Uncertainty-Guided Gaussian Learning (UGL) models pseudo-labels as probabilistic distributions, dynamically calibrating supervision intensity. Experiments on DanceTrack, EmboTrack, SportsMOT, and JRDB demonstrate that PS-Track provides a feasible and effective point-supervised alternative across diverse tracking scenarios, establishing a new state-of-the-art for point-supervised tracking. The source code is available at https://github.com/xifen523/PS-MOT.

</details>


### [243] [PGE-SAM: Prompt-Guided Feature Enhancement for Interactive Segmentation under Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30477)
*Tuan-Duc Nguyen,Anh-Tuan Mai,Duc-Trong Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PGE-SAM通过引入提示引导的特征增强、多尺度特征交互和前景重建损失，显著提升了SAM在图像退化（噪声、模糊、压缩）下的分割鲁棒性，同时保持对干净图像的泛化能力，参数仅为先前方法的五分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM在真实场景的图像退化（噪声、模糊、压缩）下性能严重下降，现有方法全局恢复特征而不聚焦于分割相关区域，且忽视了SAM的迭代精化机制，导致交互式分割表现次优。

Method: 提出PGE-SAM框架：1) 提示引导生成器(Prompt Guidance Generator)利用用户提示和先验 mask 预测在空间上引导特征恢复聚焦于感兴趣区域；2) 多尺度特征交互(Multi-Scale Features Interaction)引入低级编码器特征恢复退化下丢失的细粒度细节；3) 前景重建损失(Foreground Reconstruction Loss)将特征级监督限制在分割目标上。同时构建DM-Seg基准，涵盖多种医学图像模态和退化类型。

Result: PGE-SAM在医学和自然图像领域的多种退化级别上均达到最先进的鲁棒性，同时保持对干净图像的泛化能力，参数仅为先前方法的五分之一。

Conclusion: PGE-SAM有效解决了SAM在退化图像上性能下降的问题，通过提示引导的空间特征增强机制，在低参数开销下实现了鲁棒的交互式分割。

Abstract: Segment Anything Model (SAM) has revolutionized promptable image segmentation with strong zero-shot generalization. However, its performance degrades substantially under real-world imaging artifacts such as noise, blur, and compression. Existing methods restore features globally without focusing on segmentation-relevant regions and neglect SAM's iterative refinement mechanism, leading to suboptimal performance in interactive settings. We propose Prompt-Guided Feature Enhancement SAM (PGE-SAM), a framework that explicitly leverages user prompts and prior mask predictions to spatially guide the feature restoration process toward regions of interest through a Prompt Guidance Generator. To recover fine-grained details lost under degradation, we introduce Multi-Scale Features Interaction to incorporate low-level encoder features, along with a Foreground Reconstruction Loss that restricts feature-level supervision to the segmentation target. Furthermore, we present DM-Seg, a benchmark for interactive segmentation on degraded medical images, spanning multiple imaging modalities with both general and modality-specific degradations at varying severity levels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PGE-SAM achieves SOTA robustness on both medical and natural image domains across multiple degradation levels, while maintaining generalization to clean images and adding less than one-fifth of the parameters of prior methods.

</details>


### [244] [RBE-Flow: Recurrent Bayesian Estimation on Feature Manifolds for Cross-Modal Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30492)
*Mengzhu Ding,Xin Song,Xiaoke Ding,Hongwei Ding,Xuecong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RBE-Flow提出了一种闭环递归贝叶斯估计框架，用于解决跨模态图像配准中的非线性辐射差异和几何畸变问题。该方法通过递归流形优化块与不确定性自适应概率更新相结合，实现了密集跨模态流估计的自校正机制，并在多个基准数据集上取得了显著优于现有方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统确定性匹配方法缺乏不确定性感知能力，难以应对跨模态图像间严重的非线性辐射差异和几何畸变，导致在高度非凸的优化空间中频繁累积误差，配准精度受限。

Method: 将密集跨模态流估计建模为闭环递归贝叶斯估计问题；采用递归流形优化(RMO)块迭代生成流观测及其不确定性；通过不确定性自适应概率更新(UAPU)利用确定性sigma点投影将观测融合到先验状态；将校准后的后验协方差反馈以自适应正则化后续优化步的阻尼；引入混合监督方案，包含几何感知修正NLL损失以防止方差坍缩。

Result: 在OSdataset、WHU-OPT-SAR和RoadScene三个具有挑战性的跨模态配准基准上取得了最优性能，尤其在严格亚像素精度指标下显著超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的闭环贝叶斯递归优化框架能有效提升跨模态图像配准的鲁棒性和精度，反馈式协方差调节机制是应对多模态数据复杂畸变的关键。

Abstract: Cross-modal image registration is essential for multi-sensor perception but remains fundamentally challenging due to severe non-linear radiometric discrepancies and geometric distortions. Existing deterministic matching methods lack uncertainty awareness, struggling to navigate the resulting highly non-convex optimization landscape and frequently accumulating errors in ambiguous regions. In this paper, we propose RBE-Flow, a novel framework that reformulates dense cross-modal flow estimation as a closed-loop recurrent Bayesian estimation problem on learned feature manifolds. Diverging from standard feed-forward regression, RBE-Flow establishes a robust self-correcting mechanism by deeply coupling feature-metric non-linear optimization with probabilistic state updates. Specifically, a Recurrent Manifold Optimization (RMO) block iteratively generates flow observations and their associated uncertainties, which are then optimally assimilated into the prior state via an Uncertainty-Adaptive Probabilistic Update (UAPU) using deterministic sigma-point projection. Crucially, the resulting calibrated posterior covariance is fed back to adaptively regularize the damping of subsequent optimization steps, allowing the system to modulate its convergence based on predictive confidence. To ensure stable probabilistic training, we introduce a hybrid supervision scheme featuring a geometry-aware rectified NLL loss that structurally prevents variance collapse. Extensive experiments on challenging OSdataset, WHU-OPT-SAR, and RoadScene benchmarks demonstrate that RBE-Flow consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing methods by a significant margin, particularly under strict sub-pixel criteria. Project page: https://github.com/NEU-Liuxuecong/RBE-Flow

</details>


### [245] [On the Faithfulness of Post-Hoc Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30498)
*Laines Schmalwasser,Jan Blunk,Niklas Penzel,Julia Niebling,Joachim Denzler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析了后验概念瓶颈模型（post-hoc CBMs）中概念投影的失败模式：辅助数据下的协变量偏移和视觉语言模型生成的代理概念标签中的系统性标签噪声，导致概念投影不忠实。作者提出了解耦概念忠实度与预测准确性的新指标，实验表明这些指标能检测出标准准确性评估无法识别的非忠实行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后验CBM以目标任务准确率作为主要成功指标，掩盖了所学概念是否具有语义意义的问题。随机概念投影也能达到竞争性准确率，说明准确性无法保证概念忠实性。作者旨在解耦概念忠实度与预测准确性，识别并形式化概念投影的失败模式。

Method: 1. 形式化两种失败模式：辅助数据协变量偏移导致的概念不忠实（给出误差上界）和VLM生成代理标签的系统性标签噪声。2. 提出新指标，将概念忠实度与预测准确性解耦。3. 在真实和合成基准上进行实证验证。

Result: 新提出的指标能够识别出标准基于准确率评估无法检测的非忠实行为，在真实和合成基准上均验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 后验CBM中概念投影可能存在语义不忠实的问题，仅靠准确性评估不够，需要解耦的忠实度指标来确保概念语义有意义。

Abstract: Human decision-making interprets the world through high-level concepts, such as recognizing a bird by its belly color. To bridge the gap between opaque deep learning representations and human understanding, Post-Hoc Concept Bottleneck Models (post-hoc CBMs) project latent features onto interpretable concept spaces using auxiliary datasets or vision-language models. However, relying on target task accuracy as the primary measure of post-hoc CBM success obscures whether the learned concepts are semantically meaningful or merely predictive artifacts. For example, random concept projections can achieve competitive accuracy despite being semantically meaningless. In this work, we analyze the learned projections directly and identify two failure cases: First, for concept projections learned from auxiliary data, covariate shifts can lead to unfaithful concept representations for the target task. In particular, we provide an upper bound on the error introduced by this shift. Second, systematic label noise in surrogate concept labels generated by vision-language models leads to unfaithful projections. After formalizing these failure modes, we introduce novel metrics that decouple concept faithfulness from predictive accuracy. Our empirical results across real-world and synthetic benchmarks confirm that these metrics identify unfaithful behaviors that standard accuracy-based evaluation fails to detect.

</details>


### [246] [High-Resolution Flood Mapping With Sentinel-1 and Sentinel-2 via Misalignment-Robust Cross-Sensor Learning and Generative Despeckling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30511)
*David Ma,Jeremy Feinstein,Shreya Pandit,Arkaprabha Ganguli,Eugene Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成式洪水制图框架，通过构建覆盖美国本土的高质量Sentinel-2/Sentinel-1数据集、引入位移不变损失函数处理SAR与光学标签之间的配准误差，以及使用条件变分自编码器（CVAE）对SAR多时序复合影像进行去斑，显著提升了高分辨率洪水范围的测绘精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高分辨率卫星洪水制图受限于数据保真度和传感器特有伪影：多光谱光学影像受云、阴影和城市干扰，SAR影像受散斑噪声和配准不确定性影响，且现有基准数据集对恶劣天气和城市环境覆盖不足。

Method: 1) 构建覆盖美国本土的S2/S1像素级10m水体掩膜数据集；2) 通过严格地理空间标注协议手工生成高质量S2标注，并利用弱监督时序对齐将标签迁移至SAR影像；3) 设计位移不变损失函数容忍SAR与光学标签间的残余地理定位误差；4) 在SAR多时序复合影像上训练CVAE进行去斑，保留洪水相关空间结构。

Result: UNet和UNet++架构在光学数据上AUPRC最高达0.956；在SAR洪水制图中，位移不变损失与CVAE去斑相比经典滤波器带来统计显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 数据集保真度、对配准误差鲁棒的训练策略以及生成式去斑方法对于可操作的洪水制图具有重要价值。

Abstract: Reliable high-resolution flood extent mapping from satellite imagery remains constrained by limited data fidelity and sensor-specific artifacts. Multispectral optical imagery is degraded by clouds, shadows, and urban confounders, while synthetic aperture radar (SAR) imagery is affected by speckle noise and sensor co-registration uncertainty. This work presents an integrated flood mapping framework that jointly addresses these limitations through curated datasets and novel learning strategies. We introduce a new Sentinel-2 (S2) and Sentinel-1 (S1) dataset covering the contiguous United States, featuring pixel-accurate 10 m water masks with emphasis on challenging weather conditions and urban environments that are underrepresented in existing benchmarks. High-quality S2 annotations are manually produced using rigorous geospatial labeling protocols and transferred to SAR imagery through weakly labeled temporally coincident acquisitions. To address SAR-specific artifacts, a shift-invariant loss function is employed to tolerate residual geolocation uncertainty between SAR imagery and optical-derived labels, and a Conditional Variational Autoencoder (CVAE) is trained on multitemporal SAR composites to suppress speckle while preserving flood-relevant spatial structure. Experiments using UNet and UNet++ architectures demonstrate strong multispectral performance (AUPRC up to 0.956) and statistically significant improvements in SAR flood mapping when using shift-invariant loss and CVAE-based despeckling compared to classical filters. These results underscore the importance of dataset fidelity, misalignment-robust training, and demonstrate the viability of generative despeckling for operational flood mapping.

</details>


### [247] [3D Scene-Adaptive Trajectory-Controllable Human Image Animation with Camera Movement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30514)
*Deyin Liu,Jicheng Xu,Lin Yuanbo Wu,Xiaowei Zhao,Xiatian Zhu,Zhe Jin,Anjan Dutta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种场景自适应的人体图像动画框架，通过在重建的3D环境中同时控制人体运动和相机轨迹来生成视频，并在两个基准数据集上取得了显著优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人体图像动画方法虽已从2D骨骼/姿态升级到3D建模条件，但在自然场景中生成轨迹可控的人体运动并适应相机视角变化方面仍面临挑战。

Method: 1) 地面自适应3D运动重定向方法，实现对地面高度和朝向变化的自动适应；2) 视角自适应潜在融合机制，通过场景可见性掩码将点云几何先验注入生成过程，提供相机控制下的视角变化精确指导。

Result: 在两个标准人体图像动画基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在相关视频生成指标上显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一种场景自适应的人体图像动画框架，通过3D环境重建实现了对人体运动和相机轨迹的联合控制，有效解决了现有方法在自然场景下视角变化和轨迹控制方面的局限性。

Abstract: Human image animation, which aims to generate a video of a reference subject following a provided action sequence, has received increasing research interest. With the development of diffusion-based/flow-based video foundation models, existing animation works have began to upgrade the guidance information from 2D skeleton/pose to 3D modeling conditions. Despite achieving reasonable results, these approaches face challenges in synthesizing trajectory-controllable human motion within natural scene under changed camera views. In this work, we present a scene-adaptive human image animation framework that controls both human motion and camera trajectories within a reconstructed 3D environment for video generation. To achieve this, we first develop a ground-adaptive 3D motion retargeting approach to enable user-friendly motion trajectory control adapting to the changes of elevations of ground and orientations automatically. Then we design a viewpoint-adaptive latent fusion mechanism to inject point-cloud geometric priors through scene-visibility masking into the generative process, providing precise guidance of viewpoint changes under camera control. Experiments on two standard human image animation benchmark datasets demonstrate remarkable improvements of our method over the state of the arts in related video generation metics. Project page: https://robinhood256100.github.io/web-disp

</details>


### [248] [HASTE: A Framework for Training-Free, Dynamic, and Steerable Compression of Pre-Trained Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30516)
*Lukas Meiner,Jens Mehnert,Alexandru Paul Condurache*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HASTE是一种即插即用的卷积模块，利用局部敏感哈希在推理时动态合并冗余通道，实现无需重新训练即可压缩预训练CNN，在CIFAR-10上对ResNet34减少46.2% FLOPs，仅损失1.25%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限设备上部署大型CNN面临高计算成本的挑战，现有动态执行方法通常需要专门训练或微调，对预训练模型效果有限且需数据访问，因此需要一种无需训练、即插即用的动态压缩方法。

Method: HASTE作为即插即用卷积模块，在推理时通过局部敏感哈希（LSH）识别并合并潜在特征图中冗余的通道（patch-wise），同时压缩输入特征及其对应滤波器的深度，实现计算量更低的卷积操作。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上跨多种架构的实验表明，ResNet34在CIFAR-10上实现了46.2% FLOPs降低，准确率仅下降1.25%，且无需任何重新训练。通过消融研究验证了核心设计选择，并分析了方法的性质与局限性。

Conclusion: HASTE为预训练CNN提供了一种在运行时可控压缩的有效方案，无需重新训练，开创了高效深度学习部署的新可能性，其通道合并方案与Vision Transformer中的token合并任务概念相关。

Abstract: Deploying large convolutional neural networks (CNNs) on resource-constrained devices is challenging due to their high computational cost. While dynamic execution methods are promising, existing approaches for CNNs typically require specialized training or fine-tuning, limiting their effectiveness when applied to pre-trained models and requiring data access. To address this gap, we propose HASTE (Hashing for Tractable Efficiency), a plug-and-play convolution module that enables training-free, dynamic compression of large pre-trained CNNs. At inference time, HASTE uses locality-sensitive hashing to identify and merge redundant channels of latent feature maps on a patch-wise basis. This process simultaneously compresses the depth of both input features and their corresponding filters, resulting in computationally cheaper convolutions. We conduct extensive experiments on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet across a range of architectures, demonstrating a 46.2% FLOPs reduction in a ResNet34 on CIFAR-10 with only a 1.25% drop in accuracy, without any retraining. We support our claims by comprehensive ablation studies to validate our core design choices, an analysis of the method's properties and limitations, and a discussion that connects our channel merging scheme to the conceptually related task of token merging in Vision Transformers. Our results demonstrate that HASTE provides an effective solution for steerable compression of pre-trained CNNs at runtime, opening new possibilities for the deployment of efficient deep learning methods.

</details>


### [249] [$μ$Flow: Leveraging Average Images for Improving Generalisation of Deepfake Faces Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30528)
*Orazio Pontorno,Mattia Litrico,Luca Guarnera,Mario Valerio Giuffrida,Sebastiano Battiato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 μFlow，一种仅用真实图像训练的单类深度伪造检测器，通过平均多张图像放大生成痕迹，并利用归一化流对齐特征分布，在完全未见过的生成器上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器大多依赖有监督训练（同时使用真实和伪造图像），导致其对未见过的生成器（尤其是跨类别生成器，如GANs vs 扩散模型）泛化能力有限。为了确保实际应用中的安全性，需要一种能有效泛化到未知生成器的检测方法。

Method: 1) 观察到平均多张图像能放大一致的生成痕迹，产生高判别性的特征表示；2) 对平均图像提取的特征分布进行建模；3) 训练归一化流（normalizing flow）将单张图像的特征空间与该分布对齐；4) 通过基于似然的标准区分真实与伪造样本。

Result: 在完全分布外（OOD）的设定下（训练时真实和伪造数据集均未见），μFlow显著优于当前最先进的检测器。

Conclusion: μFlow作为一种仅需真实图像训练的单类检测方法，通过利用平均图像增强生成痕迹并结合归一化流特征对齐，实现了对未知生成器强大的泛化能力，为深度伪造检测提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Current generative models, including GANs and diffusion models, have reached an outstanding level of photorealism, posing significant risks to privacy and security. To ensure real-world applicability, deepfake detectors must generalise effectively to unseen generators. However, most existing approaches rely on supervised training with both real and fake images, which limits their generalisation especially across generators categories (e.g. GANs vs DMs). In this work, we introduce $μ$Flow, a one-class deepfake detector trained only on real images without relying on pseudo-deepfakes or synthetic artifacts. Our approach builds on the observation that averaging multiple images amplifies consistent generative traces, producing highly discriminative feature representations. We leverage this property by modelling the distribution of features extracted from averaged images and training a normalizing flow to align the feature space of individual images with this distribution. This alignment yields a likelihood-based criterion that separates real and fake samples while promoting strong generalisation. We evaluate $μ$Flow on a fully out-of-distribution setting, where both real and fake datasets are unseen during training. Experimental results show that our method significantly outperforms SOTA detectors. Project page: https://opontorno.github.io/MuFlow.

</details>


### [250] [Orca: The World is in Your Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30534)
*Yihao Wang,Yuheng Ji,Mingyu Cao,Yanqing Shen,Runze Xiao,Huaihai Lyu,Senwei Xie,Euan Liu,Klara Tian,Tianfeng Long,Yichi Zhang,Zhengliang Cai,Ruike Chen,Jifan Zhao,Ruochuan Shi,Zihan Tang,Jing Lyu,Wenxing Tan,Ningbo Zhang,Yangtao Hu,Yuming Gao,Xiansheng Chen,Junkai Zhao,Congsheng Xu,Boan Zhu,Ziqi Wang,Yupu Feng,Qiongqiong Zhang,Yingli Zhao,Yulong Ao,Shaoxuan Xie,You Liu,Guocai Yao,Leiduo Zhang,Xiaodan Liu,Yunyan Zhang,Yance Jiao,Xinyan Yang,Jiaxing Wei,Xu Liu,Tengfei Pan,Shaokai Nie,Chunlei Men,Sen Cui,Xiaojie Jin,Hongyang Li,Jianlan Luo,Yao Mu,Yunchao Wei,Jun Yan,Hang Zhao,Xiaolong Zheng,Jiaming Li,Yonghua Lin,Tiejun Huang,Zhongyuan Wang,Pengwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Orca是一个通用世界基础模型，通过Next-State-Prediction范式从多模态世界信号中学习统一的世界潜在空间，支持文本生成、图像预测和具身动作生成等下游任务，在125K小时视频和1.6亿事件标注上预训练后，冻结主干仅训练轻量解码器，性能超越同等规模专用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型通常针对单一模态或任务（如next-token、next-frame、next-action预测）进行优化，缺乏统一的、能够理解和预测世界状态的通用框架。

Method: 提出Next-State-Prediction建模范式，包含两种互补学习方式：无意识学习（从连续视频中捕获密集自然状态转移）和有意识学习（通过语言描述的事件和VQA监督建模稀疏有意义的状态转移）。构建大规模世界学习库存数据（125K小时视频+1.6亿事件标注），预训练后冻结主干，仅训练轻量模态特定解码器。

Result: 实验证明了所提出范式的可扩展性，更强的世界潜在空间带来更强的下游读出能力，Orca在三个下游任务（文本生成、图像预测、具身动作生成）上超越同等规模专用基线。

Conclusion: Orca作为通用世界基础模型，为理解、预测和作用于世界提供了一种有前景的统一方法，同时指出了当前局限以启发社区。

Abstract: We introduce Orca, an initial instantiation of a general world foundation model. Orca learns a unified world latent space from multimodal world signals and exposes it through multimodal readout interfaces. Rather than optimizing isolated next-token, next-frame, or next-action prediction, we are centered on Next-State-Prediction modeling, offering a unified state-transition modeling route toward understanding, predicting, and acting upon the world. Orca learns through two complementary paradigms: unconscious learning captures dense natural state transitions from continuous videos, and conscious learning models sparse meaningful state transitions by language-described events and VQA supervision. For pre-training, we construct a large-scale world-learning inventory data, including 125K hours of video data and 160M event annotations. After pre-training, Orca learns a unified world latent space. To examine whether the learned latent supports downstream, we evaluate it by three representative downstream readouts: text generation, image prediction, and embodied action generation. Orca's backbone is frozen, and only the lightweight modality-specific decoders are trainable. Experiments show the scalability of the proposed paradigm and verify that stronger world latent enables stronger downstream readouts. Orca outperforms similar-sized specialized baselines. These results show that Orca, as a general world foundation model, presents a promising approach to understanding, predicting, and acting upon the world. Finally, we discuss the current limitations, aiming to provide useful insights and inspiration for the community.

</details>


### [251] [StereoGS: Sparse-View 3D Gaussian Splatting via Stereo Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30545)
